00014 C       * ONLY PACGAME/PACMAP/PACAI MODIFY IT; PACDISP READS IT.
00015 C        01  PAC-STATE.
00016 C            05  MAP-AREA.
00017 C                10  MAP-ROW           OCCURS 10 TIMES.
00018 C                    15  MAP-CELL      PIC X OCCURS 20 TIMES.
00019 C            05  PLAYER-X              PIC 99.
00020 C            05  PLAYER-Y              PIC 99.
00021 C            05  TICK-COUNT            PIC 999.
00022 C            05  SCORE                 PIC 9(4).
00023 C            05  PELLETS-LEFT          PIC 999.
00024 C            05  LIVES                 PIC 9.
00025 C            05  GAME-STATE            PIC X(7).
00026 C            05  LAST-CMD              PIC X.
00027 C            05  LAST-RESULT           PIC X(7).
00028 C            05  F-WON                 PIC 9.
00029 C            05  F-LOST                PIC 9.
00030 C            05  F-QUIT                PIC 9.
00031 C            05  F-EOF                 PIC 9.
00032 C            05  F-HIT                 PIC 9.
00033 C            05  AI-LEVEL              PIC 9.
00034 C            05  GHOST-COUNT           PIC 9.
00035 C            05  GHOST-SEED            PIC 9(5).
00036 C            05  GH-ENTRY              OCCURS 3 TIMES.
00037 C                10  GH-X              PIC 99.
00038 C                10  GH-Y              PIC 99.
00039 C                10  GH-DIR            PIC 9.
00040          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00041 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00042 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00043 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00044 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00045 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00046 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00047 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00048 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00049          PROCEDURE DIVISION.
00050          MAIN-PARA.
00051              MOVE 'INIT' TO REQ-OP.
00052              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00053              MOVE 'RSET' TO REQ-OP.
00054              CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.   2


00055              MOVE 'OPEN' TO REQ-OP.
00056              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00057              MOVE 'HEAD' TO REQ-OP.
00058              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00059              PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00060              PERFORM CHECK-CONFIG THRU CHECK-CONFIG-EXIT.
00061              MOVE 'INIT' TO REQ-OP.
00062              CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00063              MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP.
00064              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00065              PERFORM TURN-PARA
00066                  UNTIL F-EOF = 1 OR F-QUIT = 1 OR F-WON = 1
00067                     OR F-LOST = 1.
00068              MOVE 'END ' TO REQ-OP.
00069              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00070              MOVE 'CLOS' TO REQ-OP.
00071              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00072              MOVE 'FINI' TO REQ-OP.
00073              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00074              STOP RUN.
00075          READ-INPUT.
00076              MOVE 'READ' TO REQ-OP.
00077              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00078              IF REQ-KIND = 'EOF'
00079                  MOVE 'EOF ' TO REQ-OP
00080                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00081          READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00082              EXIT.
00083          CHECK-CONFIG.
00084              IF REQ-KIND = 'CFG'
00085                  MOVE 'CFG ' TO REQ-OP
00086                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00087                  PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00088          CHECK-CONFIG-EXIT.
00089              EXIT.
00090          TURN-PARA.
00091              PERFORM DISPATCH THRU DISPATCH-EXIT.
00092              PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00093          DISPATCH.
00094              IF REQ-KIND = 'MOV'
00095                  MOVE 'MOVE' TO REQ-OP
00096                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00097                  MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP
00098                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00099              ELSE IF REQ-KIND = 'QUI'
00100                  MOVE 'QUIT' TO REQ-OP
00101                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00102              ELSE
00103                  MOVE 'INVL' TO REQ-OP
00104                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00105          DISPATCH-EXIT.
00106              EXIT.   3

00001         * PACGAME : GAME RULES AND STATE (PORTABLE, NO TERMINAL I/O).
00002         * OPS : RSET  DEFAULTS (3 LIVES, PLAYER 2,2, AI1, 1 GHOST)
00003         *       CFG   APPLY REQ-LEVEL / REQ-COUNT FROM THE CONFIG CARD
00004         *       INIT  LOAD MAP, PLACE GHOSTS, COUNT PELLETS
00005         *       MOVE  ONE TURN (REQ-DIR, REQ-CHAR)   QUIT  EOF
00006         * RULES : SEE DOCS/DECISIONS.MD D7, D9, D11, D14.

00009          ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.

00013          DATA DIVISION.
00014          WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.
00015          01  MAP-REQ COPY PACMREQ.
00016 C       * MAP SERVICE REQUEST: M-OP = LOAD WALL CLR  EAT  CNT
00017 C        01  MAP-REQ.
00018 C            05  M-OP                  PIC X(4).
00019 C            05  M-X                   PIC 99.
00020 C            05  M-Y                   PIC 99.
00021 C            05  M-FLAG                PIC 9.
00022 C            05  M-COUNT               PIC 999.

00024          01  GS-TABLE REDEFINES GS-DATA.

00027                  10  GS-Y          PIC 99.

00031          01  NEW-Y                 PIC 99.

00035 C       * ONLY PACGAME/PACMAP/PACAI MODIFY IT; PACDISP READS IT.

00037 C            05  MAP-AREA.

00039 C                    15  MAP-CELL      PIC X OCCURS 20 TIMES.
00040 C            05  PLAYER-X              PIC 99.
00041 C            05  PLAYER-Y              PIC 99.
00042 C            05  TICK-COUNT            PIC 999.
00043 C            05  SCORE                 PIC 9(4).
00044 C            05  PELLETS-LEFT          PIC 999.

00047 C            05  LAST-CMD              PIC X.
00048 C            05  LAST-RESULT           PIC X(7).
00049 C            05  F-WON                 PIC 9.
00050 C            05  F-LOST                PIC 9.
00051 C            05  F-QUIT                PIC 9.
00052 C            05  F-EOF                 PIC 9.
00053 C            05  F-HIT                 PIC 9.
00054 C            05  AI-LEVEL              PIC 9.   2


00055 C            05  GHOST-COUNT           PIC 9.
00056 C            05  GHOST-SEED            PIC 9(5).
00057 C            05  GH-ENTRY              OCCURS 3 TIMES.
00058 C                10  GH-X              PIC 99.
00059 C                10  GH-Y              PIC 99.
00060 C                10  GH-DIR            PIC 9.
00061          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00062 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00063 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00064 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00065 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00066 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00067 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00068 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00069 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00070          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00071          MAIN-PARA.
00072              IF REQ-OP = 'RSET'
00073                  PERFORM DO-RESET
00074              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'CFG '
00075                  PERFORM DO-CONFIG
00076              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'INIT'
00077                  PERFORM DO-INIT
00078              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'MOVE'
00079                  PERFORM DO-MOVE
00080              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'QUIT'
00081                  PERFORM DO-QUIT
00082              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'EOF '
00083                  MOVE 1 TO F-EOF.
00084              GOBACK.
00085          DO-RESET.
00086              MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-X.
00087              MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-Y.
00088              MOVE 0 TO TICK-COUNT.
00089              MOVE 0 TO SCORE.
00090              MOVE 0 TO PELLETS-LEFT.
00091              MOVE 3 TO LIVES.
00092              MOVE 'READY  ' TO GAME-STATE.
00093              MOVE ' ' TO LAST-CMD.
00094              MOVE 'START  ' TO LAST-RESULT.
00095              MOVE 0 TO F-WON.
00096              MOVE 0 TO F-LOST.
00097              MOVE 0 TO F-QUIT.
00098              MOVE 0 TO F-EOF.
00099              MOVE 0 TO F-HIT.
00100              MOVE 1 TO AI-LEVEL.
00101              MOVE 1 TO GHOST-COUNT.
00102              MOVE 12345 TO GHOST-SEED.
00103          DO-CONFIG.
00104              MOVE REQ-LEVEL TO AI-LEVEL.
00105              MOVE REQ-COUNT TO GHOST-COUNT.
00106          DO-INIT.
00107              MOVE 'LOAD' TO M-OP.
00108              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00109              MOVE PLAYER-X TO M-X.
00110              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO M-Y.
00111              MOVE 'CLR ' TO M-OP.   3


00112              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00113              PERFORM RESET-GHOST VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00114                  UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00115              PERFORM CLEAR-GHOST-CELL VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00116                  UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00117              MOVE 'CNT ' TO M-OP.
00118              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00119              MOVE M-COUNT TO PELLETS-LEFT.
00120          RESET-GHOST.
00121              MOVE GS-X (WS-G) TO GH-X (WS-G).
00122              MOVE GS-Y (WS-G) TO GH-Y (WS-G).
00123              MOVE GS-DIR (WS-G) TO GH-DIR (WS-G).
00124          CLEAR-GHOST-CELL.
00125              MOVE GH-X (WS-G) TO M-X.
00126              MOVE GH-Y (WS-G) TO M-Y.
00127              MOVE 'CLR ' TO M-OP.
00128              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00129          DO-QUIT.
00130              MOVE 1 TO F-QUIT.
00131              MOVE 'QUIT   ' TO GAME-STATE.
00132          DO-MOVE.
00133              MOVE REQ-CHAR TO LAST-CMD.
00134              MOVE PLAYER-X TO NEW-X.
00135              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO NEW-Y.
00136              MOVE 'MOVED  ' TO LAST-RESULT.
00137              PERFORM AIM-MOVE THRU AIM-MOVE-EXIT.
00138              ADD 1 TO TICK-COUNT.
00139              MOVE NEW-X TO M-X.
00140              MOVE NEW-Y TO M-Y.
00141              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00142              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00143              IF M-FLAG = 1
00144                  MOVE 'BLOCKED' TO LAST-RESULT
00145              ELSE
00146                  MOVE NEW-X TO PLAYER-X
00147                  MOVE NEW-Y TO PLAYER-Y
00148                  PERFORM EAT-PELLET THRU EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00149              PERFORM ROUND-END THRU ROUND-END-EXIT.
00150              PERFORM SET-STATE THRU SET-STATE-EXIT.
00151          AIM-MOVE.
00152              IF REQ-DIR = 'U'
00153                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-Y
00154              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'D'
00155                  ADD 1 TO NEW-Y
00156              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'L'
00157                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-X
00158              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'R'
00159                  ADD 1 TO NEW-X.
00160          AIM-MOVE-EXIT.
00161              EXIT.
00162          EAT-PELLET.
00163              MOVE PLAYER-X TO M-X.
00164              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO M-Y.
00165              MOVE 'EAT ' TO M-OP.
00166              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00167              IF M-FLAG = 1
00168                  ADD 10 TO SCORE   4


00169                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM PELLETS-LEFT.
00170              IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00171                  MOVE 1 TO F-WON.
00172          EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00173              EXIT.
00174          ROUND-END.
00175              MOVE 0 TO F-HIT.
00176              PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00177              IF F-HIT = 0 AND F-WON = 0 AND AI-LEVEL > 0
00178                  CALL 'PACAI' USING PAC-STATE
00179                  PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00180              IF F-HIT = 1
00181                  PERFORM LOSE-LIFE THRU LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00182          ROUND-END-EXIT.
00183              EXIT.
00184          CHECK-HIT.
00185              PERFORM HIT-TEST THRU HIT-TEST-EXIT
00186                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00187          CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00188              EXIT.
00189          HIT-TEST.
00190              IF GH-X (WS-G) = PLAYER-X AND GH-Y (WS-G) = PLAYER-Y
00191                  MOVE 1 TO F-HIT.
00192          HIT-TEST-EXIT.
00193              EXIT.
00194          LOSE-LIFE.
00195              MOVE 0 TO F-WON.
00196              SUBTRACT 1 FROM LIVES.
00197              IF LIVES = 0
00198                  MOVE 1 TO F-LOST
00199                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO LAST-RESULT
00200              ELSE
00201                  MOVE 'DIED   ' TO LAST-RESULT
00202                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-X
00203                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-Y
00204                  PERFORM RESET-GHOST VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00205                      UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT
00206                  IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00207                      MOVE 1 TO F-WON.
00208          LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00209              EXIT.
00210          SET-STATE.
00211              IF F-LOST = 1
00212                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO GAME-STATE
00213              ELSE IF F-WON = 1
00214                  MOVE 'WON    ' TO GAME-STATE
00215              ELSE
00216                  MOVE 'RUNNING' TO GAME-STATE.
00217          SET-STATE-EXIT.
00218              EXIT.   5

00057          01  MAP-REQ COPY PACMREQ.
00058 C       * MAP SERVICE REQUEST: M-OP = LOAD WALL CLR  EAT  CNT
00059 C        01  MAP-REQ.
00060 C            05  M-OP                  PIC X(4).
00061 C            05  M-X                   PIC 99.

00065          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00066          MAIN-PARA.
00067              MOVE 0 TO M-FLAG.
00068              IF M-OP = 'LOAD'
00069                  MOVE MAP-DATA TO MAP-AREA
00070              ELSE IF M-OP = 'WALL'
00071                  PERFORM DO-WALL THRU DO-WALL-EXIT
00072              ELSE IF M-OP = 'CLR '
00073                  MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X)
00074              ELSE IF M-OP = 'EAT '
00075                  PERFORM DO-EAT THRU DO-EAT-EXIT
00076              ELSE IF M-OP = 'CNT '
00077                  PERFORM DO-COUNT.
00078              GOBACK.
00079          DO-WALL.
00080              IF M-X < 1 OR M-X > 20 OR M-Y < 1 OR M-Y > 10
00081                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG
00082              ELSE IF MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X) = '#'
00083                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG.
00084          DO-WALL-EXIT.
00085              EXIT.
00086          DO-EAT.
00087              IF MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X) = '.'
00088                  MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X)
00089                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG.
00090          DO-EAT-EXIT.
00091              EXIT.
00092          DO-COUNT.
00093              MOVE 0 TO M-COUNT.
00094              PERFORM COUNT-ROW VARYING WS-ROW FROM 1 BY 1
00095                  UNTIL WS-ROW > 10.
00096          COUNT-ROW.
00097              PERFORM COUNT-CELL THRU COUNT-CELL-EXIT
00098                  VARYING WS-COL FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-COL > 20.
00099          COUNT-CELL.
00100              IF MAP-CELL (WS-ROW WS-COL) = '.'
00101                  ADD 1 TO M-COUNT.
00102          COUNT-CELL-EXIT.
00103              EXIT.   3

00039          01  REV-TABLE REDEFINES REV-DATA.
00040              05  REV-D             PIC 9 OCCURS 4 TIMES.
00041          01  G-NX                  PIC 99.
00042          01  G-NY                  PIC 99.
00043          01  DIST-X                PIC S99.
00044          01  DIST-Y                PIC S99.
00045          01  DIST-T                PIC S999.
00046          01  BEST-DIST             PIC S999.
00047          LINKAGE SECTION.
00048          01  PAC-STATE COPY PACSTATE.
00049 C       * SHARED GAME STATE. NO VALUE CLAUSES (USED IN LINKAGE TOO).
00050 C       * ONLY PACGAME/PACMAP/PACAI MODIFY IT; PACDISP READS IT.
00051 C        01  PAC-STATE.
00052 C            05  MAP-AREA.
00053 C                10  MAP-ROW           OCCURS 10 TIMES.
00054 C                    15  MAP-CELL      PIC X OCCURS 20 TIMES.   2


00055 C            05  PLAYER-X              PIC 99.
00056 C            05  PLAYER-Y              PIC 99.
00057 C            05  TICK-COUNT            PIC 999.
00058 C            05  SCORE                 PIC 9(4).
00059 C            05  PELLETS-LEFT          PIC 999.
00060 C            05  LIVES                 PIC 9.
00061 C            05  GAME-STATE            PIC X(7).
00062 C            05  LAST-CMD              PIC X.
00063 C            05  LAST-RESULT           PIC X(7).
00064 C            05  F-WON                 PIC 9.
00065 C            05  F-LOST                PIC 9.
00066 C            05  F-QUIT                PIC 9.
00067 C            05  F-EOF                 PIC 9.
00068 C            05  F-HIT                 PIC 9.
00069 C            05  AI-LEVEL              PIC 9.
00070 C            05  GHOST-COUNT           PIC 9.
00071 C            05  GHOST-SEED            PIC 9(5).
00072 C            05  GH-ENTRY              OCCURS 3 TIMES.
00073 C                10  GH-X              PIC 99.
00074 C                10  GH-Y              PIC 99.
00075 C                10  GH-DIR            PIC 9.
00076          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE.
00077          MAIN-PARA.
00078              PERFORM GHOST-MOVE THRU GHOST-MOVE-EXIT
00079                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00080              GOBACK.
00081          GHOST-MOVE.
00082              MOVE 0 TO CAND-N.
00083              MOVE GH-DIR (WS-G) TO WS-CUR.
00084              MOVE REV-D (WS-CUR) TO WS-REVD.
00085              PERFORM GHOST-PROBE THRU GHOST-PROBE-EXIT
00086                  VARYING WS-D FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-D > 4.
00087              IF CAND-N = 0 AND AI-LEVEL > 1
00088                  PERFORM TRY-REVERSE THRU TRY-REVERSE-EXIT.
00089              IF CAND-N > 0
00090                  PERFORM CHOOSE-DIR THRU CHOOSE-DIR-EXIT
00091                  PERFORM STEP-GHOST.
00092          GHOST-MOVE-EXIT.
00093              EXIT.
00094          GHOST-PROBE.
00095              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00096              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00097              MOVE G-NX TO M-X.
00098              MOVE G-NY TO M-Y.
00099              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00100              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00101              IF M-FLAG = 0
00102                  IF AI-LEVEL = 1 OR WS-D NOT = WS-REVD
00103                      ADD 1 TO CAND-N
00104                      MOVE WS-D TO CAND (CAND-N).
00105          GHOST-PROBE-EXIT.
00106              EXIT.
00107          TRY-REVERSE.
00108              MOVE WS-REVD TO WS-D.
00109              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00110              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00111              MOVE G-NX TO M-X.   3


00112              MOVE G-NY TO M-Y.
00113              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00114              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00115              IF M-FLAG = 0
00116                  MOVE 1 TO CAND-N
00117                  MOVE WS-D TO CAND (1).
00118          TRY-REVERSE-EXIT.
00119              EXIT.
00120          CHOOSE-DIR.
00121              IF AI-LEVEL = 3
00122                  PERFORM PICK-CHASE
00123              ELSE IF AI-LEVEL = 2 AND CAND-N = 1
00124                  MOVE CAND (1) TO WS-D
00125              ELSE
00126                  PERFORM PICK-RANDOM.
00127          CHOOSE-DIR-EXIT.
00128              EXIT.
00129          PICK-RANDOM.
00130              COMPUTE WS-WORK = GHOST-SEED * 75 + 74.
00131              DIVIDE WS-WORK BY 65537 GIVING WS-QUOT
00132                  REMAINDER GHOST-SEED.
00133              DIVIDE GHOST-SEED BY CAND-N GIVING WS-QUOT
00134                  REMAINDER WS-REM.
00135              ADD 1 TO WS-REM.
00136              MOVE CAND (WS-REM) TO WS-D.
00137          PICK-CHASE.
00138              MOVE 999 TO BEST-DIST.
00139              PERFORM CHASE-EVAL THRU CHASE-EVAL-EXIT
00140                  VARYING WS-J FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-J > CAND-N.
00141          CHASE-EVAL.
00142              MOVE CAND (WS-J) TO WS-K.
00143              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-K) - 1.
00144              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-K) - 1.
00145              COMPUTE DIST-X = G-NX - PLAYER-X.
00146              IF DIST-X < 0
00147                  COMPUTE DIST-X = 0 - DIST-X.
00148              COMPUTE DIST-Y = G-NY - PLAYER-Y.
00149              IF DIST-Y < 0
00150                  COMPUTE DIST-Y = 0 - DIST-Y.
00151              COMPUTE DIST-T = DIST-X + DIST-Y.
00152              IF DIST-T < BEST-DIST
00153                  MOVE DIST-T TO BEST-DIST
00154                  MOVE WS-K TO WS-D.
00155          CHASE-EVAL-EXIT.
00156              EXIT.
00157          STEP-GHOST.
00158              COMPUTE GH-X (WS-G) = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00159              COMPUTE GH-Y (WS-G) = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00160              MOVE WS-D TO GH-DIR (WS-G).   4

*OPTIONS IN EFFECT*     SIZE = 2097152  BUF = 1048576  LINECNT = 57  SPACE1, FLAGW,   SEQ,   SOURCE

00001         * PACINPT : INPUT ADAPTER. ONE SYSIN CARD -> ONE REQUEST.
00002         * OPS : OPEN  READ  CLOS.  READ SETS REQ-KIND:
00003         *   MOV (REQ-DIR U/D/L/R)  QUI  CFG (REQ-LEVEL, REQ-COUNT)
00004         *   INV  EOF.  REQ-CHAR ALWAYS HOLDS THE CARD'S FIRST BYTE.
00005         * ONLY THIS MODULE KNOWS ABOUT THE TERMINAL/CARD SOURCE.
00006          IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
00007          PROGRAM-ID. PACINPT.
00008          ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
00009          CONFIGURATION SECTION.
00010          SOURCE-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00011          OBJECT-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00012          INPUT-OUTPUT SECTION.
00013          FILE-CONTROL.
00014              SELECT COMMAND-FILE ASSIGN TO UT-S-SYSIN.
00015          DATA DIVISION.
00016          FILE SECTION.
00017          FD  COMMAND-FILE
00018              RECORDING MODE IS F
00019              LABEL RECORDS ARE OMITTED
00020              RECORD CONTAINS 80 CHARACTERS
00021              DATA RECORD IS COMMAND-RECORD.
00022          01  COMMAND-RECORD.
00023              05  CMD-CHAR          PIC X.
00024              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00025              05  CMD-LEVEL         PIC X.
00026              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00027              05  CMD-COUNT         PIC X.
00028              05  FILLER            PIC X(75).
00029          LINKAGE SECTION.
00030          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00031 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00032 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00033 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00034 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00035 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00036 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00037 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00038 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00039          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-REQ.
00040          MAIN-PARA.
00041              IF REQ-OP = 'OPEN'
00042                  OPEN INPUT COMMAND-FILE
00043              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'READ'
00044                  PERFORM DO-READ THRU DO-READ-EXIT
00045              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'CLOS'
00046                  CLOSE COMMAND-FILE.
00047              GOBACK.
00048          DO-READ.
00049              MOVE SPACES TO REQ-KIND.
00050              READ COMMAND-FILE AT END MOVE 'EOF' TO REQ-KIND.
00051              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES
00052                  PERFORM CLASSIFY THRU CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00053          DO-READ-EXIT.
00054              EXIT.   2


00055          CLASSIFY.
00056              MOVE CMD-CHAR TO REQ-CHAR.
00057              MOVE 'INV' TO REQ-KIND.
00058              IF CMD-CHAR = 'U' OR CMD-CHAR = 'u'
00059                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00060                  MOVE 'U' TO REQ-DIR
00061              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'D' OR CMD-CHAR = 'd'
00062                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00063                  MOVE 'D' TO REQ-DIR
00064              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'L' OR CMD-CHAR = 'l'
00065                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00066                  MOVE 'L' TO REQ-DIR
00067              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'R' OR CMD-CHAR = 'r'
00068                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00069                  MOVE 'R' TO REQ-DIR
00070              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'Q' OR CMD-CHAR = 'q'
00071                  MOVE 'QUI' TO REQ-KIND
00072              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'A'
00073                  MOVE 'CFG' TO REQ-KIND
00074                  PERFORM SET-CONFIG THRU SET-CONFIG-EXIT.
00075          CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00076              EXIT.
00077          SET-CONFIG.
00078              IF CMD-LEVEL = '0'
00079                  MOVE 0 TO REQ-LEVEL
00080              ELSE IF CMD-LEVEL = '2'
00081                  MOVE 2 TO REQ-LEVEL
00082              ELSE IF CMD-LEVEL = '3'
00083                  MOVE 3 TO REQ-LEVEL
00084              ELSE
00085                  MOVE 1 TO REQ-LEVEL.
00086              IF CMD-COUNT = '2'
00087                  MOVE 2 TO REQ-COUNT
00088              ELSE IF CMD-COUNT = '3'
00089                  MOVE 3 TO REQ-COUNT
00090              ELSE
00091                  MOVE 1 TO REQ-COUNT.
00092          SET-CONFIG-EXIT.
00093              EXIT.   3

00079          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00080 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00081 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00082 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00083 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00084 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00085 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00086 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00087 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00088          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00089          MAIN-PARA.
00090              IF REQ-OP = 'HEAD'
00091                  DISPLAY 'PACMAN MVS-6 SMART GHOSTS'
00092              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'STAT'
00093                  PERFORM SHOW-STATE THRU SHOW-STATE-EXIT
00094              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'INVL'
00095                  DISPLAY 'INVALID COMMAND ' REQ-CHAR
00096              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'END '
00097                  PERFORM SHOW-END THRU SHOW-END-EXIT.
00098              GOBACK.
00099          SHOW-END.
00100              IF F-LOST = 1
00101                  DISPLAY 'LOST'
00102              ELSE IF F-WON = 1
00103                  DISPLAY 'WIN'
00104              ELSE IF F-QUIT = 1
00105                  DISPLAY 'QUIT'
00106              ELSE
00107                  DISPLAY 'EOF'.
00108              DISPLAY 'TURNS ' TICK-COUNT.
00109              DISPLAY 'SCORE ' SCORE.
00110              DISPLAY 'LIVES ' LIVES.
00111          SHOW-END-EXIT.   3


00112              EXIT.
00113          SHOW-STATE.
00114              MOVE TICK-COUNT TO ST-TURN.
00115              MOVE LAST-CMD TO ST-CMD.
00116              MOVE LAST-RESULT TO ST-RESULT.
00117              MOVE PLAYER-X TO ST-X.
00118              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO ST-Y.
00119              MOVE SCORE TO ST-SCORE.
00120              MOVE GH-X (1) TO ST-GX.
00121              MOVE GH-Y (1) TO ST-GY.
00122              MOVE LIVES TO ST-LIVES.
00123              MOVE GAME-STATE TO ST-STATE.
00124              MOVE PELLETS-LEFT TO ST-LEFT.
00125              DISPLAY WS-STATUS-LINE.
00126              IF GHOST-COUNT > 1
00127                  PERFORM SHOW-EXTRA THRU SHOW-EXTRA-EXIT.
00128              PERFORM SHOW-ROW VARYING WS-ROW FROM 1 BY 1
00129                  UNTIL WS-ROW > 10.
00130          SHOW-STATE-EXIT.
00131              EXIT.
00132          SHOW-ROW.
00133              MOVE MAP-ROW (WS-ROW) TO WS-LINE.
00134              IF WS-ROW = PLAYER-Y
00135                  MOVE 'P' TO WS-LINE-CELL (PLAYER-X).
00136              PERFORM OVERLAY-GHOST THRU OVERLAY-GHOST-EXIT
00137                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00138              DISPLAY WS-LINE.
00139          OVERLAY-GHOST.
00140              IF WS-ROW = GH-Y (WS-G)
00141                  MOVE GH-X (WS-G) TO WS-GX
00142                  MOVE 'G' TO WS-LINE-CELL (WS-GX).
00143          OVERLAY-GHOST-EXIT.
00144              EXIT.
00145          SHOW-EXTRA.
00146              MOVE GH-X (2) TO XL2-X.
00147              MOVE GH-Y (2) TO XL2-Y.
00148              IF GHOST-COUNT = 2
00149                  DISPLAY WS-XL2
00150              ELSE
00151                  MOVE GH-X (3) TO XL3-X
00152                  MOVE GH-Y (3) TO XL3-Y
00153                  DISPLAY WS-XL2 WS-XL3.
00154          SHOW-EXTRA-EXIT.
00155              EXIT.   4
